# INVENTORY B1 — raw vtable-slot reads in runtime/, standalone/, engine/, dx11/

Block 0 of the normal-virtual cutover, question 1 (`NEXT_SESSION.md` §3b) for the four non-Game
directories. READ-ONLY inventory, written 2026-09-28 against `main` @ deaf604c (+ the uncommitted
`standalone/rt_export_addrs.inc` edit, which contains no slot read). Nothing was built or run.

**Scope.** `runtime/` (src + types), `standalone/`, `engine/`, `dx11/`. Not `code/Game`, not `tests/`
(other agents). Every class/slot claim below was read from the header named in the row; anything that
could not be settled from the code is marked NOT VERIFIED.

**Method.** Candidate lines were collected five ways and de-duplicated by `file:line` (column `src`):

| src | source |
|---|---|
| P | `partc.py --list` for "raw vt[0]", "raw vt[N", "vtable byte offset", "slot helpers", "CallSlot0", plus "dx11 seam" (190 in-scope rows) |
| B | a broader regex: any `…vt…/…table…[` index, `(*(void***)x)[N]`, `[… / 8]`, `VTableOf(`, `vt + k…` |
| H | calls of every local slot helper found (`Slot`, `VSlot*`, `RwScalar`, `ComSlot`, `CallGetTypeId`, …) and every `constexpr k…Vt…/Slot…/Rw…/Enc…` definition |
| C | every use of a named slot constant (`rw_vtbl::`, `encoding_vtbl::`, `kRw*`, `kEnc*`, `kCtrlVtbl*`, `kVt*`, `kSlot*`, `pdvt::`, `ddvt::`, `kStreamVtbl*`, …) |
| M | read by hand while following receivers (vptr loads, `ReadVtableSlot`, `CompanionSlot`, `EncCall*`, `RwSlot`, dx11 vptr stamps, identity checks) |

Each line was then classified by rules written after reading the site (the scratch classifier is
`scratchpad/b3x/inv-b1/classify.py`); 1,235 candidate lines, of which 30 were regex noise from the C
sweep (component names in strings) and are dropped, leaving **1,205 rows**.

---

## 1. Headline findings

1. **partc.py sees 190 of the in-scope rows; 623 further rows index a class that flips and are not in
   any partc list.** Almost all of them are `IO::ReaderWriter` reads spelled
   `rw->vtable[rt::io::rw_vtbl::kScalarInt / 8]` (partc's `VT\s*\[\s*k\w+` requires the constant to be
   the first token; `rt::io::…` and `… / 8` defeat it) and helper-mediated reads (`RwScalar(rw, slot, …)`,
   `ReadVtableSlot`, `RwSlot`, `CompanionSlot`, `EncCallArr`, `VTable(x)[N]`). See §6.
2. **`IO::ReaderWriter` is the size of the step in this scope: 539 rows in 78 files** (449 MEMBER, 89
   DELETE, 1 TABLE-BUILDER). Retail slot 0 is a deleting destructor (`0x180023bd0`, Serializer.h:434-437), so it is a
   FLIP class by the §3 rule; it is also its OWN hierarchy root (no `Object::Base`), so it is the one other
   class besides the twelve `*Data` classes that COULD be held on `DeletingDtor` by decision. Every one of
   the 26 slots is declared by name (Serializer.h:479-512) and `IO::Serializer`'s non-virtual forwarders
   (`serializer_class.cpp`) are already the by-name surface, so conversion is mechanical — but it is
   ~540 edits. Decide this BEFORE the flip block plan (see §2.2).
3. **Two NEXT_SESSION §3a exempt-list entries are wrong:**
   - `edgemesh_vtable.cpp:452` is NOT an IO::Encoding table: `ctx.encoding` is the ReaderWriter the
     Encoding factory returned (`:374`), and `vt[0](…, 1)` is its **delete** — a FLIP site.
   - `font.cpp:410` is NOT "UI::Font's factory-flag slot": it is `IO::Encoding::IsBinary()` on
     `stream->encodingFactory` (+0x10). Exempt, but the exempt CLASS is IO::Encoding; `UI::Font` is an
     ordinary FLIP class (Font.h:210).
   (`graphicsmesh_vtable.cpp:486/488` ARE `IO::Encoding::IsBinary` — confirmed, `enc = stream->encoding`.)
4. **A by-name delete through the wrong root:** `standalone/menudialogloopt_stubs.cpp:447-448` deletes
   `SunLight` / `MeshInstance` / the DLC ship (Geometry subclasses) via
   `reinterpret_cast<Object::Base*>(o)->DeletingDtor(1)`. `Graphics::Geometry` is its own root
   (Geometry.h:409, no base); this works today only because both roots put `DeletingDtor` at slot 0.
   After the flip `delete (Object::Base*)geom` is undefined and dispatches through the wrong layout. No
   slot-read pattern sees it — it is a by-name call. It must become `delete static_cast<Graphics::Geometry*>(o)`.
5. **Swapped names at a slot read:** `object/external_load.cpp:198/201` — helper `SerU32` calls
   `+0xa0` (= `SerializeInt`) and `SerI32` calls `+0x98` (= `SerializeUInt`); `stringtable_factory.cpp:185`
   types `+0xa0` as `SerU32Fn`. The offsets are retail's and correct; the names are swapped. A by-name
   conversion must follow the OFFSET, not the helper name.
6. **Dead helper:** `ui/description_common.h:104` `CallSlot0` has zero callers.
7. **Identity checks survive the flip:** `DEFINE_EXE_VTABLE` returns `&_ZTV…[2]` (the Itanium address
   point, code/Game/Object/exe_type.h:122-124); `splash_loop_stubs.cpp:446`, `main.cpp:885`
   (RunFactorySelfTest), `pausemenu_harness.cpp:240` and the `stress_harness.cpp` vptr hash key compare
   whole vptrs, not slots, and are unaffected. `tools/dispatch_slot_map.py` maps exe IMPORT (IAT) slots,
   not vtable slots — unaffected.

## 2. The classes, from their headers

"Slot" is the retail slot = today's compiler slot (every class below mirrors retail order). FLIP = retail
slot 0 is a deleting destructor, so the class gets `virtual ~T()` and every later slot moves +1.

| class (root) | slot 0 today | header | verdict | rows here |
|---|---|---|---|---:|
| `Object::Base` → `Internal`, `External` → `CompiledExternal` → `Video::ImageBuffer`, `IO::PackageFile`, all UI Elements/Descriptions/DialogInstance/DialogController, exe Component/Tool/Dialog tree | `DeletingDtor` | TypeInfo.h:314 (Base: [1] TypeName :318, [2] Type :325); External.h:624-630 ([3] GetFileVersion, [4] Compile, [5] Serialize, [6] Reconstruct, [7] Destruct); Internal.h:532 ([3] Persist) | **FLIP** (one hierarchy) | 41 Object + 74 UI + 9 ImageBuffer + 11 exe-side (standalone harnesses on code/Game classes) |
| `IO::ReaderWriter` → BinaryReader / BinaryWriter / Visitor | `DeletingDtor` (pure) | Serializer.h:474-512 (26 slots) | **FLIP** by the rule; its own root, so holdable — decision §2.2 | 539 |
| `IO::Stream` → MemoryStream, CompressedStream → CompressedMemoryStream, FileStream → BinaryFileStream/TextFileStream | `DeletingDtor` (pure) | Stream.h:421 ([1] GetSource :438, [2] Write :441, [3] Read :442, [4] SetCurrentPos :443, [5] GetCurrentPos :444, [6] GetSize :445, [7] Close :524/:678, [8] PreCommit :679); FileStream.h:502 ([7] Open) | **FLIP** (own root) | 48 |
| `Graphics::Geometry` → Light (AmbientLight/SpotLight/SunLight), MeshInstance, ParticleInstance, … | `DeletingDtor` | Geometry.h:439 ([2] Draw :453, [3] AddDrawItems :456, [5] GetSubTransform :463, [6] GetInstanceTransform :465, [7] SetSharedInstanceData :467, [8] Clean :469); Light.h:120/123 ([9] DrawShadowMap, [10] DrawLight) | **FLIP** (own root — NOT under Object::Base) | 11 (+4 hazard rows, finding 4) |
| `Engine::UpdateLoop` → LoaderLoop, DialogLoopT, … | `DeletingDtor` | UpdateLoop.h:319 ([7] SaveRestore :338) | **FLIP** (own root) | 2 |
| `IO::Encoding` → Binary/Text/VisitorEncoding | `IsBinary` | Serializer.h:365-376 | **EXEMPT** (no dtor slot; retail tables unexported, 5 slots) | 146 |
| `System::Thread` → Engine::Loader, Audio::Stream, … | `Run` | Thread.h:252/264 | **EXEMPT** | 2 |
| `ControllerComponent::Controller` (9 slots) | `FindState` | code/Game/Component/ControllerComponent.h:51-67 | **EXEMPT** | 2 |
| the twelve `Video::*Data` | `DeletingDtor` | CompanionData.h:209-713 | **FLIP by the rule; EXEMPT recommended** (§3) | 66 runtime + 63 dx11 |
| COM (IXAudio2*, IDirectInputDevice8, IDXGISwapChain) | `QueryInterface` | Windows SDK | NOT OURS — stays | 60 |

### 2.1 Every FLIP root above is independent

`IO::ReaderWriter`, `IO::Stream`, `Graphics::Geometry`, `Engine::UpdateLoop` and each `Video::*Data`
are roots of their own (verified: none has a base class; grep for `public Video::XData` finds no
derivation). Only `Object::Base`'s tree is the "one hierarchy, one commit" block §3b.5 describes. The
other roots can be flipped (or held) independently of it and of each other.

### 2.2 IO::ReaderWriter — the decision this inventory forces

- **RW-convert** (then flip): 449 MEMBER rows become `static_cast<IO::ReaderWriter*>(rw)->SerializeX(v, name)`
  (scalar members take `T&`, so `*p`), 89 DELETE rows become `->DeletingDtor(1)` / `delete`. The bridge
  cast is needed because most sites hold the `rt::io::ReaderWriter` POD (Serializer.h:119) — the two are
  static_asserted interchangeable (:535-537). Converting `serializer_class.cpp` first (its `RwSlot` /
  `ScalarCall` templates) turns every caller of `IO::Serializer::Serialize` into a by-name caller for free;
  the rest are the per-file "make a Serializer by hand" bodies (the `MakeReaderWriter` / `MakeCodec`
  pattern repeated in ~40 files). Behaviour-neutral; resave ×5 proves it.
- **RW-hold**: keep `virtual ReaderWriter* DeletingDtor(unsigned) = 0` at slot 0 and leave the 539 rows.
  Legal because the class is its own root and nothing derives from it outside Serializer.h. Cost: the
  goal statement ("every class with a destructor in its retail slot 0 declares `virtual ~T()`") gets a
  second exception alongside the `*Data` classes.
- Recommendation: **RW-convert, as its own block before any flip** — the by-name surface already exists,
  the guard test `a_virtual_destructor_would_shift_every_ReaderWriter_slot` (Serializer.h:461-463) pins
  today's order, and a hold would leave ~540 raw offset reads as permanent debt.

## 3. The dx11 seam (NEXT_SESSION §3b question 2)

### 3.1 The twelve classes

`runtime/types/video/CompanionData.h` declares twelve **independent root classes** in `namespace Video`,
each `{vptr, _owner}` (0x10 bytes, lower bound), each with its own `virtual XData* DeletingDtor(unsigned)`
at slot 0 (the VECTOR arms, element 0x10, `Object::detail::InternalMemFree`) and `CompanionPureCall()`
(`_purecall`) bodies in every other slot. None derives from `Object::Base` or from each other.

| class | header (class / DeletingDtor) | slots | named slots (header lines) |
|---|---|---:|---|
| DeviceData | :209 / :218 | 20 | [1] EnumerateInterfaces :231, [2] CreateDevice :232, [3] Shutdown :233, [4] Slot4 :234, [5] Slot5 :235, [6] Slot6 :236, [7] Slot7 :237, [8] SetImageBuffer :239, [9..19] Create{Context,ImageBuffer,ConstantBuffer,IndexBuffer,VertexBuffer,VertexLayout,VertexProgram,PixelProgram,RenderState,VideoProgram,RenderBuffer}Data :245-255 |
| ContextData | :263 / :272 | 20 | [1] BeginCommands … [19] EndInstancedIndexedVertices :285-309 |
| ImageBufferData | :317 / :326 | 7 | [1] AttachColorBuffer, [2] AttachDepthBuffer, [3] Create, [4] Bind, [5] Update, [6] Read :339-357 |
| IndexBufferData | :370 / :379 | 7 | [1] Create, [2] SetData, [3] Lock, [4] Unlock, [5] Bind, [6] Serialize :392-413 |
| VertexBufferData | :421 / :430 | 7 | same shape as IndexBufferData :443-452 |
| ConstantBufferData | :460 / :469 | 4 | [1] Create :482, [2] Bind :503, [3] SlotIndexPlusOne :505 |
| RenderBufferData | :513 / :522 | 4 | [1] Slot1 :535, [2] Bind :536, [3] Resolve :537 |
| VertexLayoutData | :545 / :554 | 3 | [1] Bind :567, [2] PlatformCreate :570 |
| VertexProgramData | :578 / :587 | 3 | [1] PlatformCreate :600, [2] Bind :601 |
| PixelProgramData | :609 / :618 | 3 | [1] PlatformCreate :631, [2] Bind :632 |
| RenderStateData | :640 / :649 | 3 | [1] Create :662, [2] Bind :663 |
| VideoProgramData | :675 / :684 | 3 | [1] Slot1 :697, [2] Bind :713 |

Total 84 slots (`dx11/types/VideoDX11Data.h:555-570` `kSlots::kTotal == 84`).

### 3.2 How the tables are built and stamped

- Each dx11 porting lane registers one function per (class, RETAIL slot) with `DX11_REGISTER_SLOT(Vt, N, fn,
  label)` (dx11_seam.h:132-136): 84 registrations, including one slot-0 deleting-destructor body per class
  (`*_ScalarDeletingDtor` / `*_Dtor`, e.g. buffers.cpp:1062, devicedata.cpp:2186, smallclasses.cpp:248/255).
- `Dx11SeamInstall()` (dx11_seam.cpp:143-190, called from standalone/main.cpp Init step 4b) copies them into
  `s_ourVt[12]` — `struct OurVtable { void* rtti; void* slot[20]; }` — refusing any hole, and
  `Dx11LiveVtable(Vt)` returns `&s_ourVt[vi].slot[0]`.
- The dx11 factories (`devicedata.cpp:2015 AllocData`, `exports.cpp:101`, and the per-class ctors/dtors at
  buffers.cpp:627/716/833, contextdata.cpp:440, devicedata.cpp:1127, imagebufferdata.cpp:402,
  renderstatedata.cpp:391, shaderprograms.cpp:86/207, smallclasses.cpp:77/132, vertexlayoutdata.cpp:105)
  construct the Runtime base through the `??0XData` door (`::new (self) XData(owner)`, companion_data.cpp)
  and then overwrite `vptr` with the dx11 table. The dx11 "concrete classes" are PODs
  (`dx11/types/VideoDX11Data.h`), NOT C++ subclasses of `Video::XData` (CompanionData.h:186-192, RULE B5-8).
- dx11 also builds a 4-slot DeviceData-shaped `g_dx9PlaceholderVtable` (devicedata.cpp:1951) for the
  "DirectX 9" interface entry, and dispatches into its OWN DeviceData by retail index
  (contextdata.cpp:671/673, `kSlot_Shutdown` / `kSlot_CreateDevice`, VideoDX11Data.h:130-158).

So every live `*Data` object wears a table laid out in **MSVC/retail order**: slot 0 = one deleting
destructor taking `flags`, then the named slots.

### 3.3 Who dispatches into a `*Data` object

- **By name (compiler slot)** — e.g. `reinterpret_cast<Video::DeviceData*>(dd)->Shutdown()` /
  `->CreateDevice()` / `->EnumerateInterfaces()` / `->DeletingDtor(1)` (device_startup.cpp:608/611/689/707/
  709/794/797, enumerate_video_modes.cpp:153/160, video_resources.cpp:381/525),
  `ContextData->DeletingDtor(1)` (context_lifecycle.cpp:484), `ImageBufferData->Update` (saveload_saveinfo.cpp:256,
  saveload_update.cpp:318), `Index/VertexBufferData->Unlock` (companion_data.cpp:244/255),
  `RenderBufferData->Slot1()` (device.cpp:464), `DeviceData->Slot5()` (spritecontext_flush.cpp:325),
  `RenderStateData->DeletingDtor(1)` / `->Bind` (renderstate.cpp:209), and the Video::Context thunks into
  `ContextData` (context.cpp, context_bind.cpp, context_ctor.cpp, engine/loops.cpp,
  ui/spritecontext_flush.cpp, graphics/wave27_lane_gfxpart.cpp). A heuristic grep finds **~50 such lines in
  17 runtime files** (not exhaustive; these are by-name, so outside this table's KIND rules).
- **By retail index (raw)** — **66 rows in this table** (55 MEMBER, 7 DELETE, 3 TABLE-BUILDER, 1 declaration)
  across 22 runtime files: `pdvt::` / `ddvt::` (pixelprogram.cpp, vertexprogram.cpp), `kDeviceDataCreate*`
  (vertexlayout.cpp, videoprogram.cpp), `RtVideo::kSlot*` (Context.h:734-744), `CompanionSlot<Fn>`
  (dynamic_resources.cpp), `VSlot(ctxData, 0x90/0x98)` (wave27_lane_gfxpart.cpp), `VTable(cd)[4]`
  (wave27_lane_gfxbig.cpp:1372), `vt[4]`/`vt[7]`/`vt[12]`/`vt[1..3]`/`vt[0x30/8]` in graphics/,
  loops.cpp:1111/1131, userinterface.cpp:779, mainloop_video.cpp:627, saveload_update.cpp:2049, and the
  64-slot fake ContextData oracle table in context_bind.cpp:693-711.
- **Slot 0 (deletes)**: by name at the 7 `DeletingDtor(1)` lines above; raw at vertexlayout.cpp:245,
  videoprogram.cpp:178, pixelprogram.cpp:301, vertexprogram.cpp:351, dynamic_resources.cpp:234,
  mainloop_video.cpp:627, saveload_update.cpp:2049. All land on dx11's slot-0 body with `flags = 1`.

Today both kinds agree only because compiler order == retail order for these classes.

### 3.4 The two options, with exact cost

**(A) The twelve `*Data` classes stay on `virtual XData* DeletingDtor(unsigned)` (exempt).**
Possible: each is a hierarchy root with no C++ subclass anywhere (dx11's concretes are PODs), so holding
them does not constrain any other class's flip. Cost: **zero code changes.** The 66 raw rows, the ~50
by-name lines, the 84 dx11 registrations, `kSlot_*`, `kSlots::*`, the dx9 placeholder, the context_bind
oracle all stay valid. Bookkeeping only: the §3e acceptance "0 DeletingDtor declarations / 0
DeletingDtor(1) calls outside the derived exempt list" must list these 12 declarations and their 14
slot-0 call sites (7 by name, 7 raw); a `static_assert(!std::has_virtual_destructor<Video::XData>::value)` per class (the
Thread.h:337 pattern) would pin the decision. Carry-over 6 unchanged.

**(B) The twelve flip and the seam re-indexes.** Every item below changes, in ONE commit (Runtime by-name
dispatch and dx11's tables must agree at every instant):
1. CompanionData.h: 12 `DeletingDtor` → `virtual ~XData()`; every declared slot moves +1 (DeviceData and
   ContextData become 21 slots).
2. dx11_seam: `kVtables[].slots` +1 each (84 → 96), `kMaxSlots` 20 → 21, and the table must now hold TWO
   destructor entries per class in Itanium order: [0] complete-object dtor `void(XData*)`, [1] deleting dtor
   `void(XData*)` with **no flags argument** — so each of the 12 slot-0 bodies (`*_ScalarDeletingDtor`,
   `*_Dtor`) splits into two functions with new signatures (the vector arm `flags & 2` disappears).
3. All 84 `DX11_REGISTER_SLOT(Vt, N, …)` lines: N → N+1 for N≥1, plus 12 new registrations.
4. `VideoDX11Data.h` `enum DeviceDataSlot` (`kSlot_*`, :130-158) +1, `kSlots::*` +1 each (`kTotal` 96);
   contextdata.cpp:671/673 follow.
5. `g_dx9PlaceholderVtable` (devicedata.cpp:1951) re-shaped to 5 entries.
6. The 66 raw Runtime rows: each index/byte-offset +1/+8 (or converted to by-name) — `pdvt::`/`ddvt::`
   (ShaderProgram.h:469-486), `kDeviceDataCreate*`, `RtVideo::kSlot*` + its static_asserts (context.cpp:222-230),
   `CompanionSlot` byte offsets, the `vt[N]` literals listed above, the 64-slot oracle's FakeSlot indices.
7. Raw slot-0 deletes (7 rows) become `delete static_cast<Video::XData*>(p)` — which calls compiler slot 1
   of a dx11-built table, i.e. item 2's new deleting body.
8. `DeviceData::Slot4()` must gain the `bool` parameter retail passes (loops.cpp:1103-1106) if its two raw
   callers convert.
Estimated: ~12 class edits, ~96 registration lines, 12 split dtor bodies, 2 enums, ~66 runtime rows, one
oracle table — and a dx11 change gated by `devlost.sh` as well as resave, with a failure mode (a slot off
by one) that the build cannot detect.

**Recommendation: (A).** The flip buys nothing for these classes — nothing ever deletes a `*Data` through
a Runtime base pointer to an object the Runtime built (the live table is always dx11's), the base-class
table is only ever "the vptr between two stores" (CompanionData.h:166-174), and the retail-order table is
precisely what the dx11 seam exists to reproduce. (B) re-derives 96 slots across two binaries-worth of code
in one commit for zero behavioural gain. Pin (A) with per-class `!has_virtual_destructor` static_asserts
and name the twelve in B3X_RULES' exempt list.

## 4. Special-attention items

**Stamping / copying vtable pointers (all in scope).** dx11's 12+ `vptr =` stamps (TABLE-BUILDER, stay
under A); `graphics/wave24_lane_gfxmat.cpp:314/327` (a 16-slot fake **ReaderWriter** table for the gfxmat
oracle, slot 0 records the delete flag — must be reshaped or dropped when `:612/:741` become `delete`);
`video/context_bind.cpp:693-711` (64-slot fake ContextData for an oracle, stays under A);
`standalone/dlgctrl_route.cpp:291/297` (`kSpecs[]` :165-168 hard-codes DialogController slots 5/6/10 and
byte offsets 0x28/0x30/0x50 and, when armed, WRITES thunks into the live compiler table — all +1 after the
flip); `standalone/redzone_alloc.cpp:348-349` patches allocator function pointers, not vtables.

**Comparing slot pointers.** `standalone/scene_watch.cpp:454` (`vt[3] == AddDrawItems` port address —
becomes vt[4]; better replaced by a vptr compare against MeshInstance's `_ZTV` address point);
`standalone/entity_comp_validator.cpp:89-90` (`vt + 0x10` = Type(); becomes `vt + 0x18`);
`standalone/food_placement_harness.cpp:243` (22-slot coverage map against a RETAIL-order list — shifts by
one silently); `standalone/main.cpp:538` (crash-log dump of 13 raw slots — stays, but the reader must know
the new layout); `save/saveload_update.cpp:1155` and `ui/widgets_layout.cpp:228` hand a raw slot address to
the oracle recorder `out.Call(label, fn, …)` — converting the read means giving the recorder the member.

**Header inline helpers (every includer inherits them).**
- `runtime/types/object/Internal.h:318` `InternalBasePtr::DeletePtr()` — Object::Internal slot 0 DELETE on
  the `RtObject::Internal*` POD; used by every `InternalPtr<T>` teardown. → `Object::Destroy` now, `delete`
  after (its own comment: converts when `_ptr` becomes `::Object::Internal*`).
- `runtime/types/io/Serializer.h:952-956` `IO::rw_stream::Slot/Write/Read/SetPos/GetPos` — IO::Stream slots
  2/3/4/5 by byte offset on `void*`.
- `runtime/src/ui/description_common.h` — `DestroyReaderWriter` (:141-144, RW slot 0), `RwScalar` (:148-150,
  RW slot by caller), `kEnc*`/`kRw*` (:81-86), dead `CallSlot0` (:104); used by ~20 `*description.cpp`.
- `runtime/src/ui/element_common.h:93-112` — `VSlot<Fn>(o, INDEX)` and `kSlot*` in Element order.
- `runtime/src/ui/widget_common.h:67/104-109/319` — `VTable()`, `kVt*` byte offsets, `InlineGetPaddedSize`.
- `engine/funcs/engine_funcs.h:410` `FindComponentByTypeId` — Type() at `vtable[2]` on exe Components.
- `runtime/types/video/ShaderProgram.h:469-486` `devicedata_vtbl` / `programdata_vtbl` (Data: stays under A).
- `runtime/types/io/Stream.h:329` `kStreamVtblCloseSlot = 7`, `FileStream.h:410` `kFileStreamVtblOpenSlot = 7`.

KIND legend: **DELETE** slot 0 with flag 1 (→ `Object::Destroy(p)` / `p->DeletingDtor(1)` now, `delete p`
after) · **DESTRUCT** slot 0 with flag 0 (→ `this->~T()`) · **MEMBER** a named virtual (→ by-name call;
constants and helper bodies that exist only to index are counted here too) · **EXEMPT-TABLE** a table
whose slot 0 is not a destructor (stays) · **NOT-A-VTABLE** false positive · **DIAGNOSTIC** reads a slot
only to print/compare/record it · **TABLE-BUILDER** builds, stamps or patches a table by index.

Of partc's 190 in-scope rows: MEMBER 97, DELETE 38, TABLE-BUILDER 25, EXEMPT-TABLE 14, DESTRUCT 6,
DIAGNOSTIC 5, NOT-A-VTABLE 5 (by class verdict: FLIP 123, EXEMPT 59, not ours 3, none 5).

## 6. What partc.py misses in this scope

### 6.1 Rows on a FLIP class that no partc list contains

| area | rows on a FLIP class that partc.py does not list |
|---|---:|
| runtime/src/ui | 216 |
| runtime/src/graphics | 118 |
| runtime/src/io | 93 |
| runtime/src/video | 52 |
| runtime/src/audio | 32 |
| runtime/src/object | 28 |
| standalone | 25 |
| runtime/src/system | 10 |
| runtime/src/achievement | 9 |
| runtime/src/save | 9 |
| runtime/src/collision2d | 8 |
| runtime/src/picking | 7 |
| runtime/types/io | 7 |
| runtime/src/engine | 4 |
| runtime/src/pathfinding | 3 |
| runtime/src/math | 2 |
| **total** | **623** |

(The per-site table marks every one: its `src` column has no `P`.)

### 6.2 The shapes it misses, and a regex for each

| # | shape (example) | why partc misses it | suggested pattern (add to `PATTERNS`, code lines) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `rw->vtable[rt::io::rw_vtbl::kScalarInt / 8]` (≈400 lines, 60+ files) | `VT\s*\[\s*k\w+` needs the constant first; `rt::io::` and `/ 8` defeat it | `r'\b\w*(?:vt\|vtbl\|vtable\|Vtable\|VTable)\w*\s*\[[^\]]*/\s*(?:8\|sizeof\s*\(\s*void\s*\*\s*\))\s*\]'` |
| 2 | named slot constants passed to a helper: `RwScalar(rw, rw_vtbl::kScalarInt, &x)`, `VSlot(rw2, kRwDeletingDtor)`, `Slot(src, kSlotWrite)`, `EncCallArr(enc, kEncSerializeU16Arr, …)` | no index expression on the line | `r'\b(?:rw_vtbl\|encoding_vtbl\|programdata_vtbl\|devicedata_vtbl\|pdvt\|ddvt)::k\w+\|\bk(?:Rw\|Enc\|CtrlVtbl\|DlgVtbl\|Vt[A-Z]\|StreamVtbl\|FileStreamVtbl\|InternalVtbl\|StreamSlot\|DeviceDataCreate)\w*\|\bEL::kVtbl\w+\|\bkSlot(?:Write\|SetCurrentPos\|PreCommit\|DeletingDtor\|TypeName\|Type\|ComputeSize\|ComputeLayout\|Draw\|Update\|Enable\|Contains\|SetColor\|GetBorder)\b\|kExternalSerializeSlotBytes'` |
| 3 | byte offset through a `uint8_t*`/`char*` cast: `*(void**)((uint8_t*)vt + 0xa0)`, `(char*)enc->vtable + kEncSerializeUInt`, `(const uint8_t*)vt + EL::kVtblType` | the `vt + 0x..` pattern needs the bare identifier before `+` | `r'reinterpret_cast\s*<\s*(?:const\s+)?(?:uint8_t\|char\|unsigned char)\s*\*\s*>\s*\(\s*[\w>.\-]*(?:vt\|vtable\|vtbl\|vptr)\w*\s*\)\s*\+'` |
| 4 | a helper that RETURNS the table, then indexed: `VTable(obj)[3]`, `VTable(src)[3]`, `RwVTable(rw)[k… / 8]`, `VtblOf(obj)[…]` | `\)\s*\[\s*0\s*\]` only for slot 0; `***>(…)[N]` only for the cast form | `r'\b(?:VTable\w*\|VtblOf\|Vtbl\|RwVTable\|VtOf)\s*\([^;]*?\)\s*\['` |
| 5 | slot helpers whose names are not `VSlot/SlotOf/VCall*/CallVSlot/VtSlot*/SlotFn*`: `Slot<Fn>(obj, 0x28)`, `Slot(s, off)`, `SourceSlot`, `RwSlot`, `ReadVtableSlot`, `CompanionSlot<Fn>`, `ComSlot`, `ComCall`, `EncCallUInt/EncCallArr`, `DeletingDtorOf`, `GetTypeIdVirtual`, `Slot7`, `SerializeVirtual`, `CallGetTypeId`, `CallStreamClose*`, `RenderStateSlot4`, `IssueDraw`, `SerializeBufferSlot`, `DestroyReaderWriter`, `DeleteRw`, `InjectClick` | name list | extend the helper alternation with `(?:\w*Slot\w*\|DeletingDtorOf\|GetTypeIdVirtual\|SerializeVirtual\|CallGetTypeId\|EncCall\w+\|ComCall\|CallStreamClose\w*\|DestroyReaderWriter\|DeleteRw)\s*[<(]` (then triage: `Slot` also matches IAT helpers such as collection/alloc_door.cpp) |
| 6 | `(*static_cast<void***>(obj))[slot]` (compressed_stream_read/write) | pattern requires `reinterpret_cast`-free `***` form followed by a literal `[1-9]` | `r'\(\s*\*\s*(?:static_cast\|reinterpret_cast)\s*<\s*void\s*\*\*\*\s*>\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\)\s*\['` |
| 7 | a by-name slot-0 call through the WRONG root: `reinterpret_cast<Object::Base*>(geom)->DeletingDtor(1)` (menudialogloopt_stubs.cpp:448) | not a slot read at all | not a regex problem: for every `DeletingDtor(1)` / `Object::Destroy` site, check that the cast target is the object's own root (`Object::Base`, `Graphics::Geometry`, `IO::Stream`, `IO::ReaderWriter`, `Engine::UpdateLoop`, `Video::XData`) |

False positives partc produces in scope (all listed in the table as NOT-A-VTABLE): the three CRC
`static uint32_t table[256]` (crc.cpp:48, persistence_worker_win.cpp:171, persistence_saveinfo_job.cpp:187),
`combodescription.cpp:173/211` (`table` is the StringTable, +0x28 its count), `tool_harness.cpp` log lines.

## 7. Per-site table

Short forms in the last-but-one column:
- **L-RW** — `static_cast<IO::ReaderWriter*>(rw)->Member(…)` (the member named in the row). Obstacle: the
  receiver is the `rt::io::ReaderWriter` POD or `void*` (one bridge cast, interchangeable per
  Serializer.h:535-537); scalar members take `T&`, so pass `*p`.
- **L-RWD** — `static_cast<IO::ReaderWriter*>(rw)->DeletingDtor(1)` now, `delete rw` after. Obstacle: POD/`void*` receiver.
- **L-ENC** — stays (IO::Encoding has no destructor slot); optionally `static_cast<IO::Encoding*>(e)->CreateReader/CreateWriter/IsBinary(…)`.
- **L-COM** — stays: a COM interface, not a class of ours.
- **L-DATA** — stays raw under option A (the dx11-owned retail-order table); a by-name call is also correct under A.

`fn` is the nearest enclosing definition found by a heuristic scan (approximate). `code` is trimmed; the
backquote character is shown as `'`. `src`: P partc · B broad regex · H helper/constant sweep · C constant-use sweep · M by hand.
